## Measured branch boundaries and reversal divergence

Three branches reach period1 output repetitions under this rule: intact כוידא forward at pass7 (first repeat8); whole-total כוידא full-glyph-reverse at pass63 (first repeat64); intact וידא full-glyph-reverse at pass90 (first repeat91). Other21 branches have100 distinct outputs. Repetition determines the next program/state under the fixed rule, but is not a source end marker.

The earlier equality between the two reverse modes does not continue: first different outputs occur at passes17 and17 for הגה seed32 (parts and total);22 and15 for הגה seed40;11 and20 for וידא;12 and15 for כוידא. boundary_audit.json retains both exact programs and vectors at each first divergence.

The candidate start contexts are explicitly selected inputs; a source-defined beginning or ending has not emerged in this experiment. Whole-signal coverage is not established: the inherited numeric scope starts at source rows58–61 channel2, then operates on selected generated containers. Continuing these branches does not ingest the remaining grid cells. The100-pass observation limit is recorded rather than treated as an end marker.

Independent recomputation matched all2160 new pass records. All29511 new operations were checked against the preserved original instruction interpreter. Added boundary_audit.json; updated this report, the combined text, and the manifest to include these findings.
